if modified, remove any hp:lineSegArray and hp:lineSeg descendants.\n- Preserve all non-XML bytes and ZIP entry order.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport re\nimport zipfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom lxml import etree\n\nTEMPLATE_PATH = Path(\"/root/inventory_report_template.hwpx\")\nDATA_PATH = Path(\"/root/inventory_data.json\")\nOUTPUT_PATH = Path(\"/root/inventory_report_ready.hwpx\")\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}\")\n\n\ndef flatten_json(obj, prefix=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Flatten nested dicts into dot-separated keys.\"\"\"\n    out = {}\n    if isinstance(obj, dict):\n        for k, v in obj.items():\n            key = f\"{prefix}.{k}\" if prefix else str(k)\n            out.update(flatten_json(v, key))\n    elif isinstance(obj, list):\n        # Not specified by contract, but keep a predictable behavior.\n        # Flatten list items by index: a[0] -> \"a.0\".\n        for i, v in enumerate(obj):\n            key = f\"{prefix}.{i}\" if prefix else str(i)\n            out.update(flatten_json(v, key))\n    else:\n        out[prefix] = \"\" if obj is None else str(obj)\n    return out\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str | None, replacements: dict[str, str]):\n    \"\"\"Replace all {{key}} occurrences in a text string.\"\"\"\n    if text is None or \"{{\" not in text:\n        return text, 0\n\n    count = 0\n\n    def _repl(m: re.Match):\n        nonlocal count\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key in replacements:\n            count += 1\n            return replacements[key]\n        # Leave unknown placeholders unchanged; verifier will catch leftovers.\n        return m.group(0)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(_repl, text)\n    return new_text, count\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    if tag is None:\n        return \"\"\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef iter_paragraphs_with_descendant(e):\n    \"\"\"Yield (paragraph_element, descendant_element) pairs for all descendants under paragraphs.\"\"\"\n    for p in e.iter():\n        if localname(getattr(p, \"tag\", \"\")) == \"p\":\n            for d in p.iter():\n                yield p, d\n\n\ndef remove_lineseg_cache(paragraph_el):\n    \"\"\"Remove layout cache elements under a modified paragraph.\"\"\"\n    to_remove = []\n    for d in paragraph_el.iter():\n        ln = localname(getattr(d, \"tag\", \"\"))\n        if ln in (\"lineSegArray\", \"lineSeg\", \"linesegarray\", \"lineseg\"):\n            to_remove.append(d)\n\n    removed = 0\n    for node in to_remove:\n        parent = node.getparent()\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(node)\n            removed += 1\n    return removed\n\n\ndef main():\n    data = json.loads(DATA_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    replacements = flatten_json(data)\n\n    # Namespace map: the template uses the hp prefix.\n    # We only need it for XPath/element matching if used; localname() is primary.\n\n    total_replacements = 0\n    modified_paragraph_ids = set()\n    removed_cache_nodes = 0\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as zin:\n        names = zin.namelist()\n\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, \"w\", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n            for name in names:\n                raw = zin.read(name)\n\n                if not name.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                    zout.writestr(name, raw)\n                    continue\n\n                # Parse XML (bytes) and replace placeholders in text/tail.\n                try:\n                    root = etree.fromstring(raw)\n                except Exception:\n                    # If parsing fails, pass-through unchanged.\n                    zout.writestr(name, raw)\n                    continue\n\n                # Track which <hp:p> elements were modified.\n                modified_paragraphs = set()\n\n                # We need to touch .text and .tail for every element. To know which paragraph\n                # is affected, we walk descendants grouped by paragraph.\n                for p_el, d_el in iter_paragraphs_with_descendant(root):\n                    # d_el.text\n                    new_text, c1 = replace_placeholders(d_el.text, replacements)\n                    if c1:\n                        d_el.text = new_text\n                        total_replacements += c1\n                        modified_paragraphs.add(p_el)\n\n                    # d_el.tail\n                    new_tail, c2 = replace_placeholders(d_el.tail, replacements)\n                    if c2:\n                        d_el.tail = new_tail\n                        total_replacements += c2\n                        modified_paragraphs.add(p_el)\n\n                # Also handle any text/tail that might exist outside paragraphs (unlikely,\n                # but safe). We do NOT remove lineSeg caches for those.\n                for el in root.iter():\n                    if localname(getattr(el, \"tag\", \"\")) == \"p\":\n                        continue\n\n                    new_text, c1 = replace_placeholders(el.text, replacements)\n                    if c1:\n                        el.text = new_text\n                        total_replacements += c1\n\n                    new_tail, c2 = replace_placeholders(el.tail, replacements)\n                    if c2:\n                        el.tail = new_tail\n                        total_replacements += c2\n\n                #
